For those: the `npgallery.nps.gov/GetAsset/<uuid>` PDF a web search for the property name finds (check the first lines: one "Sullivan County Courthouse" result was Indiana's; Troy's 2018 to 2023 forms run to 312 pages, so save `page.py --all -n 900000` to a file and grep the inventory by street address); the State Review Board PDFs (`parks.ny.gov/sites/default/files/<yyyy-mm>/SRB_...pdf`, new forms with text layers, searched in `research/pages/` with a short regex); or the Governor's release that announced the nomination (search "governor.ny.gov nominations State and National Registers" with the name). The SHPO's own form PDFs at `parks.ny.gov/documents/shpo/national-register/...` that web search lists answer 404 (Neemes Foundry, St. George's Masonic Temple, GE Buildings 31 and 32). Where no form reads, Wikipedia's Register-based articles carry most of the detail, and HMdb nearby lists carried most of a St. Lawrence County batch.
- Utica: the Downtown Genesee Street nomination (NPGallery asset 835149cd-a9aa-483a-952c-aeee4858abd1) is longer than `page.py` keeps even with `-n 200000`; search the cached copy for "Property name:", which starts each entry with address, date and architect. Many downtown buildings have no article (the Savings Bank, the Olbiston, St. John's, the Masonic Home), so it is the best single source.
- **Register lists:** Peekskill has its own list ("... listings in Peekskill, New York"), left out of "northern Westchester County". Buffalo's list points land on the building for most downtown listings (Shea's fell on a lot behind the theatre). Rensselaer County's list gives "no coordinates" for many recent Troy listings. In northeastern Dutchess the HMdb nearby list returned the same hundred markers whatever the centre (mostly 1930s "Site of" markers with nothing to see) and the Register list (`wiki_raw.py --rows -g` with town and hamlet names) gave nearly every place; HMdb lists for a hilltown return many one-line 1932 state markers, which make leads but seldom a place.
- **State land:** NY State Parks pages moved to `parks.ny.gov/visit/historic-sites/<name>-state-historic-site` and `parks.ny.gov/visit/state-parks/<name>`, and the old `historic-sites/<name>/details.aspx` addresses are 404 (one session, earlier, still read them); the Grafton Lakes master plan and Sterling Forest trail-guide PDFs search results list are 404. DEC unit pages (`dec.ny.gov/places/<unit-name>`) give trail lengths, climbs and fire-tower status (Debar Mountain Wild Forest covers Azure, Loon Lake and Debar); `-g` returned nothing, but `--all -n 200000` into a file and a grep worked. The William C. Whitney page is `william-c-whitney-wilderness-and-round-lake-wilderness`. DEC's fire tower study (`extapps.dec.ny.gov/docs/lands_forests_pdf/ftowerstudy.pdf`, 406 pages) has a note on every Adirondack tower; its 2002 draft plan for Saratoga and Warren state forests (`.../saratogawardraftump.pdf`) has a cultural-resources section (the Granger glass factory, cemeteries); brownfield applications (`extapps.dec.ny.gov/data/DecDocs/<site>/...`) give a site's past uses with dates.
- The Register points (and Wikipedia infoboxes) for the Wakely and Snowy fire towers sit at the trailhead lots, 3 to 4 km from the summits; `nom.py "<Mountain>, New York"` gives the peak. The Mohawk-Hudson and Empire State trails at Green Island and Cohoes are not on the federal land map, so the pin check prints CHECK ACCESS there.
- NYC DEP's "open recreation areas" PDF (`nyc.gov/assets/dep/downloads/pdf/recreation/open-rec-areas-maps.pdf`) lists every watershed unit with a Y/N for Public Access Area (no permit). The Chenango Land Trust says land it owns outright is not open to the public: its canal locks are ask-first.
- **NPS:** the Saratoga hours page (`nps.gov/sara/planyourvisit/hours.htm`) gives every unit's season; the Schuyler House is `nps.gov/places/schuyler-estate.htm`. `nps.gov/upde/planyourvisit/hours.htm` covers the Roebling bridge, toll house, towpath and river accesses, whose places pages (`/places/ten-mile-river-access.htm`) give the lot point. Women's Rights' hours page is drawn by script; its FAQ (`nps.gov/wori/faqs.htm`) has the visitor center hours, and no readable page gave the house tour schedule.
- **Canals:** the American Canal Society's "BRC index with GPS" PDF gives a point for every Black River Canal lock from Rome to Lyons Falls (Lyonsdale and Port Leyden matched the LiDAR exactly); its Genesee Valley sheet (`americancanalsociety.org/wp-content/uploads/2019/08/Genesee-Valley-Canal.pdf`) is an empty form. eriecanal.org (Frank Sadowski's Traces pages, `eriecanal.org/Lock<n>.html`, `MacedonPalmyra.html`) says what is left of each enlarged lock and who owns it. Frances Dumas's "Along the Outlet of Keuka Lake" in the Crooked Lake Review (`crookedlakereview.com/articles/1_33/...`) covers every mill site on the Outlet (lock list in the April 1990 part); keukaoutlettrail.org is drawn by script, and the Land Trust's gofingerlakes.org page names the maintainer.
- **Churches:** the Episcopal Diocese of Albany's parish pages (`albanyepiscopaldiocese.org/<name>-church-<town>-ny/`, county list at `/447-2/`) give address, history and building dates, sometimes service arrangements (All Saints Chapel near Morris opens only for 10 a.m. services on the last Sunday of June, July and August); when they give no times, episcopalassetmap.org often does (check the street: a search for "St. Paul's" offers other towns' nodes). The Catholic Diocese of Albany: `https://www.rcda.org/parishes/parish/<name>-<town>-ny` (e.g. `st-josephs-church-troy-ny`) gives current Mass times when a parish site is gone. Upper New York Methodists: `unyumc.org/find-a-church/<town>-umc` worked once ("Main Service" often blank) but answered 404 or reset in three other sessions. The Maronite eparchy's `stmaron.org/parishes-n` has a "Liturgical Schedule" for every parish. The Churches of Rochester site (served as packetduck.duckdns.org) has a page for each Catholic church in the Rochester diocese, Elmira included. `hours.py page https://www.qasrcc.org/mass-times` read Queen of All Saints, Brooklyn. The Evangelist's 2018 archive (evangelist.org) reads.
- **Albany and the Capital District:** Historic Albany Foundation's "Places in Peril" page (`historic-albany.org/places-in-peril-2024`) gives address, date, architect, owner, last occupancy and condition per building, the best source for derelict buildings in Albany: try it first (two web searches for a street address gave only property listings). `-g` returns fragments: strip the tags from the cached file and search for "Owner:" (or `page.py -g "<street>"`). Albany Rural Cemetery's hours are at `albanyruralcemetery.org/about/for-visitors/hours-of-operation/`. The Albany County Historical Association's New Scotland tour PDFs are scans with no text. albanyhilltowns.com reads with the web fetch tool: "Berne - Then and Now", "Berne visitor attractions and facilities", "Churches of the past" and "Historic Homes (Knox)" list what stands. The Altamont Enterprise's `node/<n>` addresses read in full (one 2005 fairground article was behind its view limit). Menands (menandsny.gov) answers 403 to `page.py` but read with the web fetch tool once (no archived copy another time); its Sage trail map is a plain JPEG (`/wp-content/uploads/2020/11/trail-map-main.jpg`). The Town of Colonie's parks page (`colonie.org/parks/parks/`) has Schuyler Flatts' hours (read round "Schuyler Flatts Cultural Park has"). Mechanicville's historian articles (`mechanicvilleny.gov/<Title>`) read (several `node/<n>` addresses are 404). Cohoes, Waterford, Watervliet and Green Island are nearly exhausted for Register and HMdb lists: go to the Clifton Park and Halfmoon edges or to church histories.
- **Troy and Rensselaer County:** the RPI building histories (`archives.rpi.edu/institute-history/building-histories/<slug>`, index `archives.rpi.edu/node/3563`; slugs like `approach`, `87-gymnasium`); the Lansingburgh Historical Society (`lansingburghhistoricalsociety.org/<page-name>`, a page per church, firehouse, factory and cemetery, mostly dated newspaper items, listed from its archives-am and archives-nz pages); the Hudson Mohawk Industrial Gateway (`hudsonmohawkgateway.org`, textile factories and foundries with uses as of 2020); East Greenbush's Natural Resources Inventory "Historic Sites Legend" PDF (`cms2.revize.com/revize/eastgreenbushny/...`, 28 buildings) and the North Greenbush historian's columns (`townofng.com/sites/default/files/2024-05/`, northgreenbush.gov); the Sand Lake Historical Society's marker page (`sandlakehistory.org/historical-markers/index.html`; strip the tags from the cached copy); the Hoosick "Eye on History" panels on HMdb (2025, a hamlet history each with what stands; read the whole inscription). Dougtone's 2009–2010 Commons photographs along NY 7 and NY 66 show the text of markers HMdb lacks (Hoag's Tavern). troyirish.org (Irish parishes building by building) answers 403 but read with the web fetch tool once. The Rensselaer Plateau Alliance (now titled "Hudson Taconic Lands") pages for Barberville answer 404 or are drawn by script.
- **Schenectady:** Schenectady HistoryForge (`schenectady.historyforge.net/buildings/<n>`) gives building-by-building descriptions; schenectadyhistory.org (`/buildings/<town>/index.html`, `/<town>/history.html`) lists each town's HABS-photographed buildings.
- **Saratoga and Washington counties:** the Saratoga County History Center's article index (`brooksidemuseum.org/?p=12228`) links about 60 town historians' PDFs (grep the cached page for `uploads/...pdf`; read the first lines with `--all -n 400`; they gave the Colonial Inn, East Line, Leland House, Rowland's Hollow); `brooksidemuseum.org/visit/` has its hours in one line. The Town of Milton historian's listing (cms8.revize.com, "Historic Structures and Places Committee Listings.pdf", with per-hamlet PDFs) says what can still be seen. `kayadeross.org/<year>/...` articles read. GlensFalls.com's buildings pages (`glensfalls.com/history/buildings/<slug>/`, slugs on the index) cover 16 downtown buildings. The Town of Salem's landmark tour (`townofsalemny.gov/landmark-tour/`) gives date, address and fabric for the district. Lakes to Locks Passage: `passageport.org/places-to-go/<name>/` carries season, hours, fees and manager; tour pages `passageport.org/journey/<tour>/<n>-<slug>/` read with `--all -n 40000` (the article starts after "Henniker, NH"; "Travel Tools" gives parking, ownership, sometimes a point). The Washington County Historical Society (`wchs-ny.org/the-wing-northup-house/`) gives its research hours; skenesborough.com's hours were for 2021. ballstonspa.gov moved (every `/churches/pages/...` and `/plaques-dates/...` address is 404), as did saratogacountyny.gov's `?p=` addresses and greenfieldny.gov's 2025–2026 news.
- **Mohawk Valley and central:** Montgomery County's historian articles (`montgomerycountyny.gov/web/sites/departments/historian/articles.asp`) are dated (the 1828 county farm stood on the present jail's grounds on NY 5S: nothing left). mohawkvalleymuseums.us gives days and times for the Old Stone Fort (its own site gives only season and fees), the Schoharie Colonial Heritage Association and the Oneida County History Center (oneidacountyhistory.org is drawn by script). Rome Historical Society's `romehistoricalsociety.org/historic-sites` gives ownership, visiting rules and coordinates for Fort Bull, the Unknown Soldiers tomb and the Lower Landing. St. Johnsville's site is `sjvny.org`. Syracuse University's building pages (`library.syracuse.edu/special-collections-research-center/university-archives/buildings/<name>/`) give dates, architects, materials and changes. TourCayuga listings are drawn by script, but `page.py --all https://tourcayuga.com/wp-json/wp/v2/listing/<id>` reads them (the id is the `?p=` number; `?search=<word>` lists matches). chenangocounty.org/historic-museums.php has every county museum's hours. Broome County's carousel page (`broomecountyny.gov/community/carousels`) gives address, phone and season for all six. greatamericanstations.com reads (amtrak.com does not); thestanley.org has no hours page.
- **North Country and Adirondacks:** the SLCHA Quarterly back issues (`slcha.org/wp-content/uploads/quarterly/...pdf`) are good on St. Lawrence County industry. The Fort Drum gate page (`home.army.mil/drum/index.php/my-fort/all-services/gate-information`) reads (the history-tours page is 404). Watertown's library pages (`watertown-ny.gov/Genealogy315785-7711`, `/VirtualTour`) read. Visit Oswego County's historical guide (`visitoswegocounty.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/12/HistoricalGuideFNL_web.pdf`) lists every museum and Underground Railroad site with an address (its "no longer exist" symbols are lost in the text). Hamilton County's `experienceouradirondacks.com` lists small societies with hours. Saranac Lake: the museum's brochure on cdn.lakeplacid.com and the LocalWiki copy (`localwiki.org/hsl/<Page_Name>`) read. Plattsburgh's ABPP battlefield report (`cityofplattsburgh-ny.gov/sites/cityofplattsburgh.com/files/city-historian/ABPP2887-16-009FinalDrafte311.pdf`) describes Fort Brown, Fort Moreau, Fort Scott, Crab Island and Valcour Island as found in 2018.
- **Western New York:** alleganyhistory.org has articles on single buildings; Monroe County's pages read (every cityofrochester.gov page is 403); `libcal.lib.rochester.edu/hours` gives every University of Rochester library's week. recreation.gov's Mount Morris Dam tour page gives the season (army.mil and dvidshub.net are 403). The Library of Congress JSON (`https://www.loc.gov/item/<id>/?fo=json`) answers but holds little more than the title.
- **Hudson Valley:** Historic Hudson Valley's old `hudsonvalley.org/historic-sites/sunnyside/` now serves a photograph (the page is `/historic-sites/washington-irvings-sunnyside/`; hours sit in one long line, search "Friday-Sunday"). The Fishkill historian (`fishkill-ny.gov/town-history.html`) and Wappinger (`townofwappingerny.gov/historic-sites-museums/`) pages list public sites with addresses and often hours. Kingston: the Friends of Historic Kingston Stockade walking tour (`fohk.org/wp-content/uploads/2016/07/StockadeIWT2016wMAP-3.pdf`, 50 buildings) and the Hurley Heritage Society's (27 houses) give date, builder and address; the NPS Kingston itinerary is 404; `geo.py` returns both Wall Street and South Wall Street for a number, so take the PointAddress in the Stockade. The Trail Conference's hike pages read at `secure.nynjtc.org/hike/print/<n>` (404 without `secure.`) with a trailhead point and the ruins passed. Warwick's society is `whsny.org`. pickocny.com gives Orange County park hours (orangecountygov.com is 403). The Hudson River Valley Heritage Area's page on the Neversink Valley Museum reads. Sullivan County park pages read at `https://sullivanny.gov/Departments/ParksRecreation/<Name>` (DelawareHudson, StoneArchBridge, LivingstonManor, Minisink, FortDelaware) and its 2024 brochure covers all seven; sullivanoandw.com gives O&W trailheads and rules. bethelwoodscenter.org answers 406 except `/visit` (the museum hours). Westchester County Parks pages read; its PDF maps come back as HTML to a plain download, but `page.py` reads their text.
- **Long Island and the city:** easthamptonhistory.org/visit-us/ lists the month's open days (`/clinton-academy/` reads, `/mulford-farm/` is drawn by script); the Village of East Hampton reads at `easthamptonvillage.gov/<page>/`. The Town of Huntington's "Famous People & Places" folder (`huntingtonny.gov/content/13747/99540/16499/default.aspx`, PDF addresses in the cached HTML) has a history sheet for most old buildings. `wmho.org/properties/` gives the Stony Brook Grist Mill's hours; Thompson House is now shown by the WMHO, not Preservation Long Island. montaukhistoricalsociety.org answers 403 but read with the web fetch tool (its home page still speaks of reopening Second House in 2023). nypl.org branch pages often show a dated week from a past year: check the date. The Historic Districts Council's survey pages date from about 2009, so "not designated" or "closed" can be out of date (Stone Avenue designated 2015, Harlem 2021).
- **Gone to spam (do not use):** theharlemvalleynews.net, histassocsouthjeff.org, hopewelldepot.org, theschoharienews.com (November 22, 2026).
- **Refusing `page.py`** (403 unless noted): whiteface.com, orda.org, lakeplacidlegacysites.com, wellsmemoriallibrary.org, adirondackdailyenterprise.com (429), historicsaranaclake.org (resets), holycrosspbg.org, battleofplattsburgh.org (resets), fwhmuseum.com, fortwilliamhenry.com, horiconny.gov, adkhistorycenter.org and porthenrymoriah.com (no connection), shermanfreelibrary.org (no text), nycourts.gov, nysed.gov (503), albanyny.gov (resets; same to the web fetch tool, November 23, 2026), stannholytrinity.org, buffalorising.com, delawarevalleyartsalliance.org, huntersynagogue.org (no connection), shandaken.gov (drawn by script), cayugacounty.us, fingerlakes1.com's 2026 articles (2025 ones read), historicgeneva.org, sfhistoricalsociety.org, senecamuseum.com, waterloony.com, waterloohistory.org (no connection), museumships.us, newyorkalmanack.com, spotlightnews.com (429), stissinghouse.com and the Millbrook Historical Society (drawn by script), clctrust.org, hudsonhall.org (historichudson.org's Bronson House page is 404), lyonsfallslibrary.org, northporthistorical.org, cheneylibrary.org (drawn by script), villageofhoosickfalls.com, townofhoosick.org/historical.php (404), yaddo.org (yaddogardens.org no connection), nashuacitystation.org, savethepinebush.org and edinburgny.gov (drawn by script), nysm.nysed.gov (no connection), cobleskilltimesjournal.com, hendricks.syracuse.edu (no connection), syr.gov, historicoldstjohnschurch.com, chapmanmuseum.org, queensburyny.gov, chemungvalleymuseum.org, msaviour.org, yatescountyhistory.org (no connection), stgeorgesschenectady.org (resets), teslasciencecenter.org, 27east.com (429), livingnewdeal.org, obrm.org, neversinkmuseum.org, orangetourism.org, thrall.org (no connection), flowermemoriallibrary.org, hillholdandbrickhouse.org, montauklighthouse.com (and the web fetch tool), chenangohistorical.org, townofsherburne.net (404), thepost.org (no connection), the City of Binghamton's carousel page, hodgsonruss.com's 2017 news (404).

### Newfoundland and Labrador
- The Provincial Historic Sites all have a page at `https://www.seethesites.ca/sites/<name>/` with season, hours, parking and fees; `plan-your-visit/` gives season and fees for all, and next season's dates out of season, when the site pages say only "Closed for the season". Quidi Vidi Battery is no longer on its list. `.../sites/trinity/` returned an image's bytes; the Trinity Sites page is `https://www.seethesites.ca/?p=37`.
- heritagenl.ca (Heritage Foundation of NL) has a page for each provincial and municipal designation at `https://heritagenl.ca/?p=<n>` (a web search "heritagenl.ca <name>" finds the number). It is newer than the Register (it alone said Ridley Office was demolished in May 2026), and its "Google map" link carries a `q=lat,lon` that was on or beside the building every time: grep the cached page for `q=[-0-9.]*,[-0-9.]*`. Prefer it, then the ArcGIS PointAddress: Register list points were up to 4 km off (Harbour Grace Court House), 2 km (Heyfield United, in woods; Mallard Cottage).
- OpenStreetMap had named buildings for most of St. John's but is thin in Conception Bay North (`nom.py` found 5 of 26; `parking.py` almost no lots).
- Parishes seldom have pages of their own; the dioceses list churches with a phone number only: expect `?`.
- Refusing: heritage.nf.ca (resets on every request; search snippets were empty for most queries), destinationstjohns.com (403).

### North Carolina
- historicsites.nc.gov pages give hours, fees and tour times for every state historic site with `page.py --all`, and ncparks.gov park pages do too; the slugs are not the site names (House in the Horseshoe is `/all-sites/house-horseshoe`; a web search found it, guesses are 404).
- catawbahistory.org/hours-admission gives day-by-day hours for Murray's Mill, Bunker Hill Covered Bridge and the Catawba County museums.
- Hope Plantation's Wikipedia point (36.1775, -77.01917) is about 16 km north of the house; geocoding 132 Hope House Road, Windsor, was closer. Check Wikipedia points in eastern North Carolina against an address.
- biltmore.com's hours page says only "open every day" (times drawn by script). saintlawrencebasilica.org and omnihotels.com answer 403; the Diocese of Charlotte's parish page gives the basilica's Sunday Masses.

### North Dakota
- SHSND site pages (history.nd.gov) read fully with `page.py --all`, but the slugs are not obvious: Former Governors' Mansion `fgm`, Fort Rice `rice`, Cannonball Stage Station `cannonballss`, Bismarck-Deadwood Stage Trail `bdst`; `dilts`, `writingrock`, `chateau`, `pembina` work, `stevenson`, `seward`, `writing` do not. The full list is in the drop-down on `history.nd.gov/historicsites/index.html` (grep the cached page for `value="/historicsites/`). The smaller sites (Oak Lawn, Wadeson, Hudson, Ransom) print a menu block first: read after "Subscribe to SHSND podcasts."
- Wikipedia points can be well off (Cote barn, Old Main); `nom.py "<name>, <town>, North Dakota"` found Old Main at once, but finds almost nothing in rural North Dakota, where the ArcGIS geocoder's POI matches (Fort Ransom SHS, Sims Lutheran Church, Bagg Bonanza Farm) and SHSND street addresses worked.
- ndtourism.com answers 403; a web search result quoting the Lisbon listing was the only way in.

### Nova Scotia
- The Wikipedia list's `lat`/`lon` hold the Register point, which can be far off: the Argyle court house 25 km (43.5735, -65.7678), the de Gannes-Cosby House about 33 km (44.4427, -65.3045), Uniacke House's Wikipedia point 290 m, St. Andrew's Presbyterian, Lunenburg, on another house. The ArcGIS PointAddress was right for the first two (but put 50 Dugway Road, Lequille, 2 km off as a StreetAddress, where the list point was better); OpenStreetMap had Uniacke and most Lunenburg County churches and museums by name (`osmnear.py ... buildings -g Andrew` found the church).
- museum.novascotia.ca pages for single museums answer 404; each museum has its own host (clifton.novascotia.ca, uniacke.novascotia.ca) whose /visit page lists fees but showed no clock times. cardingmill.novascotia.ca serves the Museum's general pages (its /node/89 was a Parrsboro event); the Wile mill's hours were on the Town of Bridgewater's page. fortpointmuseum.com gives no hours on any page read.
- The Anglican Diocese of NS and PEI keeps a page per parish under `nspeidiocese.ca/<region>/parish-of-…` (some with service times, all with the parish's own site if any); `curl` the region page to list them.
- There is no 1 m LiDAR for Nova Scotia: the pin check runs on the aerial only.

### Ohio
- Register lists: Cincinnati's are split into "downtown", "eastern" and "western Cincinnati" (the plain title has no rows); Cleveland's is one page, "National Register of Historic Places listings in Cleveland, Ohio" (293 rows); Toledo has none of its own (the Lucas County list holds it).
- NPGallery forms are placeholders for every Muskingum County form tried (courthouse, post office, churches, the lock, the depot) and several downtown Toledo ones (Secor Hotel, Toledo Traction power station).
- ohiohistory.org answers 403 for every site page (Great Circle, Octagon, Flint Ridge; no archived copy); partners carry the hours: hopewellearthworks.org (the Octagon, Fort Ancient), mariettamuseums.org (Campus Martius). ODNR park pages come back as an empty shell ("No content found"); the Marblehead Lighthouse Historical Society's site has the tower hours, and the Muskingum River Parkway map PDF on dam.assets.ohio.gov reads (the water trail PDF is 404).
- Remarkable Ohio marker pages carry the whole state's list first; the marker's text and latitude and longitude are at the very end.
- Carnegie libraries: Eira Tansey's University of Cincinnati pre-print gives opening dates and costs for all nine Cincinnati branches, and `chpl.org/about/history/<branch>/` the rest; the Toledo library's architecture site (tlcplarchitecture.omeka.net) has every branch, retired ones included; cpl.org shows a bot check, but the Encyclopedia of Cleveland History (case.edu/ech) and Cleveland Historical cover Cleveland's.
- Springfield: herr2ck.blogspot.com has detailed 2025 posts on most of downtown's old buildings with present use (Lagonda Club, Shawnee Hotel, Masonic Temple, Third Presbyterian, Westcott House). The St. Rose Family of Parishes bulletins (irp.cdn-website.com PDFs) give Mass times for all its churches; add `2>/dev/null` to `page.py`, which prints pages of fontTools warnings.
- friendsofmusichall.org has the Music Hall tour details (cincinnatiarts.org answered 406). stjameszanesville.org refused `page.py` but read with the web fetch tool.
- Refusing: mckinleymuseum.org (a browser check to both tools), bearsmill.com (resets), jampd.com (drawn by script), millcreekmetroparks.org (no connection), Springfield News-Sun (429), toledoblade.com (navigation only, both tools), stnickparish.org and the Zanesville library's hours page (both tools).

### Oklahoma
- Register forms on NPGallery read well as text, the 1984 ones and all of Oklahoma County included (the OCU campus form's OCR spells the Gold Star building "Goldbug"); 94001083 (Guthrie Armory) is a placeholder and 100011493 and 100012781 return the 893-byte picture. County lists are quick with `wiki_raw.py --rows`; many Panhandle and Woods County rows have no coordinate.
- Oklahoma Historical Society hours are on `okhistory.org/sites/visit-<site-with-hyphens>` (visit-fort-gibson, visit-fort-towson, visit-sod-house, visit-hunters-home), not the site's main page; the site menu in any cached page lists every address.
- The 2003 Downtown Tulsa walking tour PDF (`d1pk12b7bb81je.cloudfront.net/okdataengine/appmedia/documents/0/830/walkbrochurebw.pdf`) dates fifty downtown buildings; its years differ from Wikipedia's by a year or two for several, so give both.
- NPS `nps.gov/places/<name>.htm` pages cover the Route 66 places (Coleman Theatre, Fort Reno, Arcadia Round Barn); colemantheatre.org and bostonavenue.org answer 403. fortreno.org carries a planted casino advertisement in its text: ignore it.

### Ontario
- Parks Canada: Fort Mississauga has no hours page and lives under `fortgeorge/culture/fort-mississauga`; Point Clark is under `lhn-nhs/on/clark`; the Windmill has none. Rideau lockstation pages (`parks.canada.ca/lhn-nhs/on/rideau/visit/posteeclusage-lockstation/ecluse-lock-<name>`) read with `page.py --all -n 3000` and give the civic address and a GPS point; ignore their "Constructed: 1908-1912" line, a template slip (the canal opened in 1832). The history pages are `.../histoire-history/histoire-decluse-lock-history/<name>` (`kingstonmills`, `jonesfalls`).
- The web fetch tool read Wikipedia and town sites where `page.py` was reset on parks.canada.ca and ontarioheritagetrust.ca; ask it for the specific facts in the prompt.
- hamiltoncivicmuseums.ca carries Dundurn's hours (hamilton.ca answers 403).
- Refusing or no hours: brantford.ca (404), lennox-addington.on.ca (403), bytownmuseum.com (resets), forthenry.com, kingstonhistoricalsociety.ca (Murney Tower), kingstonpentour.getanchor.io (empty, 404 or 530).

### Oregon
- Oregon State Parks publishes every park's name, point and status note as JSON at `https://stateparks.oregon.gov/services/park.cfc?method=getAll` (one plain request); park pages (`https://stateparks.oregon.gov/index.cfm?do=park.profile&parkId=<id>`) read well. The status note sometimes holds the only hours (Heceta Head lighthouse). Its points and Wikipedia's differ by up to 1.3 km (Pete French Round Barn): look at both.
- Portland: a video's standing buildings were nearly all on the Register list for South and Southwest Portland, so `wiki_raw.py --rows -g` on it settles most names in one call.
- Museum sites often block `page.py` (clatsophistoricalsociety.org 403, Klamath County's page 404; geisergrand.com and bakercountyor.gov drawn by script or refused); a web search quoting the official page was the only route, so say so in the access note. `sos.oregon.gov/archives/exhibits/ghost/...` pages are 404.

### Pennsylvania
- The state's leaf-off server is slow: 60 seconds to several minutes a tile, often timing out to Esri. Run `pincheck.py --lidar` in the background from the start, on named ids in groups of two or three, with no timeout; one session ran six copies of the workspace side by side.
- SAH Archipedia (`sah-archipedia.org/node/<n>`) reads cleanly with `page.py --all`; its "Materials:" line settles red brick and gives current use.
- The D&L heritage-corridor panels on the Lehigh Gorge Trail give exact positions for the lock ruins.
- Chester County Parks pages did not turn up in search; ironandsteelheritage.org has short pages for most iron sites in the county. mychesco.com's park features read like composites: use them only for what other sources back up. eastpikeland.org answers 403, millpictures.com 409, no9mine.com 403.
- oldeconomyvillage.org and the PHMC page give different weekday hours for Old Economy Village.
- The leaf-off pin check took about a minute a tile (15 minutes for 20 tiles, 10 for 21 without LiDAR): start it in the background as soon as the data file builds. Side-by-side copies each ran in their own folder of symlinks to the workspace (build, cache, dist, lidar, src, test, tools) with their own `research/`, so the `pincheck_picked_*` sheets did not clobber each other.
- SAH Archipedia entries by number, each read with `page.py --all` (title in the cached page's `<title>`, text between "Print Source" and "Writing Credits"): Erie `https://sah-archipedia.org/buildings/PA-01-ER1` to about ER40; Harrisburg `PA-02-DA1` to DA30 (DA5, North Front Street, covers the YMCA, the Civic Club and the mansions); Pottsville and Schuylkill `PA-02-SC1` to SC24; Scranton `PA-02-LK1` to about LK48 (LK45 does not answer).
- Scranton's landmark-buildings PDF (`scrantonpa.gov/docs/HARB/...Landmark%20Buildings...pdf`, 115 pages) ends each entry with its latitude and longitude, just before the next "CITY OF SCRANTON HISTORICAL ARCHITECTURAL REVIEW BOARD" header; STYLE and MATERIALS lines follow each history.
- historicbethlehem.org's `/visit-us/museums-sites/<name>/` pages give full weekly and seasonal hours (older addresses are 404). lvhistory.org (Lehigh Valley Passport to History) lists almost every Lehigh Valley museum with hours and fees, but it is a consortium's listing, so its hours stayed out of `hours=`. jacobsburg.org is not the Jacobsburg Historical Society (that is jacobsburghistory.com, whose home page gives no hours).
- The Schuylkill County Historical Society's site gives no hours; its newsletters (PDFs on `orca-marlin-583a.squarespace.com`) print them in the masthead. Joyce Tice's Tioga County site (joycetice.com) quotes old Wellsboro Gazette articles in full.
- Churches: the Diocese of Erie's parish pages (`eriercd.org/Parish<n>-<Name>.html`) give Mass times and address (St. Peter Cathedral is `Parish10317`); the Archdiocese of Philadelphia's parish page (`archphila.org/parish/...`) gives the cathedral's Masses.
- Carnegie libraries: Philadelphia's nomination PDF (`phila.gov/media/20200109130220/Historic-District-Carnegie-Libraries.pdf`) gives architect, builder, style, wall material and Register date for every surviving branch; the Free Library's current pages are `libwww.freelibrary.org/locations/<branch>` (the old `branches/branch.cfm` hours are stale). The Carnegie Library of Pittsburgh holds branch hours in a script.
- Refusing: historicpittsburgh.org (a human check), theburgnews.com, chaddsfordhistory.org, newlingristmill.org, uplandboro.org (403), thomasmasseyhouse.org (resets; web searches gave titles only), lehighcounty.org (no connection), saintanthonyschapel.org (403; stanthonyschapel.org does not resolve), cathedralphila.org, phlvisitorcenter.com (403).

### Prince Edward Island
- Rural Register points are usually near the building but land on road junctions, lots, lawns or woods for rural churches and others (6 of 25 in one batch: Sturgeon, Elmira, Souris church and lighthouse, Panmure, All Saints; also Tignish, Mont-Carmel, Ramsay, Moase; Point Prim 1 km off), and the ArcGIS PointAddress, often OpenStreetMap too, gives the same spot, so the geocoder adds nothing: look at every one. `nom.py` had good points for most museums and lighthouses. In Summerside most points were within 20 m, but the PEIR Armoury's was 120 m off and Trinity United's 40 m; `osmnear.py <lat> <lon> 250 buildings` settled both.
- `look.py` at 50 to 70 m half-width gives DOWNLOAD FAILED for PEI; 100 m works and picks out a lighthouse by its shadow.
- Hours: peimuseum.ca (the Museum and Heritage Foundation's seven sites) and lgpei.ca answer 403. Tourism PEI attraction pages (`tourismpei.com/attractions/<slug>`, or `tourismpei.com/search/OperatorDetails/op_id/<n>` when the slug is 404; search results give the op_id) give "Dates of operation" but never times (its Montague Welcome Centre page answered 403); museumspei.ca gives "Seasonal" or "Open year round". The City of Summerside's site (summerside.hosted.civiclive.com) answered 403; culturesummerside.com's "at a glance" page (`/copy-of-what-s-on`) gives every city museum's hours.
- The Diocese of Charlottetown's Mass-times PDF (`dioceseofcharlottetown.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/Masstimesfec2426.pdf`) reads, but the day columns run together: only a parish with a single time (Sturgeon) or a full row can be read with confidence.

### Quebec
- Parks Canada slugs are not guessable: Manoir Papineau `qc/manoirpapineau`, the canal `qc/canalcarillon` (`qc/carillon` is the Barracks), Fort Témiscamingue `qc/obadjiwan-temiscamingue`. Pointe-au-Père's hours page is drawn by script and shows only the late-season dates.
- historicplaces.ca statements for Quebec come in French even under /en/; "Description of Historic Place" gives address, dates and materials, "Character-Defining Elements" the interior fittings.
- Register points were off the building in 8 of 21 places; `nom.py` and `osmnear.py ... buildings` found the right buildings every time.
- Tourisme Mauricie (`tourismemauricie.com/en/directory/<name>-2`) and Destination Québec cité (quebec-cite.com) listings carry the operator's own website (and hours page) as links in the cached page (`grep -oE 'href="https?://[^"]+"' research/pages/<file>`): use them to find the operator, not as an hours source.
- Churches: web searches for service times returned only directories. Archdiocese of Quebec church pages (`ecdq.org/eglise-<name>/`) give the address and link the parish's mass page (end of `page.py --all`, or grep the raw HTML for "Horaire des messes"). Rural parishes now say Mass every second week in each church and publish dates by month or season: record the pattern and say the dates go stale. Montreal: `hours.py page` read the basilica, the Oratory, the Marguerite Bourgeoys Museum, the archdiocese, Pointe-à-Callière and the City; mcgill.ca/redpath answered 429 but `/redpath/visit` read.
- Hijacked by gambling pages (do not link): auxtroiscouvents.org (Château-Richer convent museum), moulin-petitpre.com.
- Refusing: sepaq.com (403, no usable archive), valjalbert.com/horaire-et-tarifs (bot check), moulinlegare.com (no answer), pulperie.com (415).

### Rhode Island
- The Historical Preservation Commission's town survey PDFs (`preservation.ri.gov/.../survey_pdfs/<town>.pdf`) read with `page.py -n 400000`; search the cached copy with a short regex for one place's paragraph.
- cathedralprovidence.org now carries spam; the Providence cathedral's site is providencecathedral.org.
- Historic New England's pages answer 403 (the Clemence–Irons page has no archived copy); the Friends of Hearthside "visit-folder" page carried the Arnold House season and the Saylesville meeting house times.
- Refusing: provlib.org (403 to both tools), trinityrep.com (403), ppacri.org (a guessed path 404).

### Saskatchewan
- `start.py` lists Saskatoon as the start city and Canada has no leads layers, so the briefing is empty: begin from the Wikipedia national-historic-site list. "List of historic places in Saskatchewan" splits on `{{HPC row` like Alberta's; its rows have no `location=`, so filter by `lat=`/`lon=` in a box; Regina, Moose Jaw and Saskatoon have their own lists.
- Register points were off at 4 of 14 places (Fort San by 250 m; Indian Head, Atwater, Arcola by more) and often 200 to 250 m off in the Qu'Appelle valley towns (Fort Qu'Appelle station, Qu'Appelle town hall, St. Peter's); the Wikipedia list gives Grey Owl's Cabin the park townsite's point, 40 km from Ajawaan Lake. `nom.py` found the station as "Fort Qu'Appelle railway station" and the Motherwell stone house as "Lanark Place, Abernethy", not under their register names.
- Saskatoon: heritage-register pages open at `saskatoon.ca/heritage-register/<slug>` (name in lower case with hyphens); the listing page answers 404 to a script, but the printed register (heritageregistry_final.pdf, linked from search results) summarises every entry. The ArcGIS geocoder gave PointAddresses for every street address tried; `nom.py` found only buildings with their own names.
- Parks Canada (Batoche) and Sask Parks (`saskparks.tourismsaskatchewan.com/...`) pages are drawn by script; the Museums Association listings (`saskmuseums.org/places/...`) give season and hours in plain text. Tourism Saskatchewan listings are mostly menu (useful text near the end; Steele Narrows read, Holy Trinity and Wood Mountain Post came back empty).
- `pincheck.py --lidar` prints NO 1 m LiDAR HERE on every Saskatchewan tile: run it without `--lidar`.
- greatwesternbrewing.com reset the connection and its archived copy could not be fetched.

### South Carolina
- The state's own register site (`schpr.sc.gov`) answers 403; NPGallery's text forms read well. historicbeaufort.org answers 403 and heritagelib.org's old addresses 404.
- Register list points were off at Sams Plantation (in marsh), Fort Howell (160 m away, by a road junction) and the Church of the Cross (on the shore); the LiDAR sheet showed the forts plainly.
- southcarolinaparks.com uses short slugs (`/hampton`, `/redcliffe`, `/rose-hill`, `/colonial-dorchester`); `page.py --all` then shows the HOURS and ADMISSION blocks, which `-g` splits.
- `nom.py` finds buildings well where the Wikipedia point is 80 to 450 m off (Hampton Plantation House, Pompion Hill Chapel, Hagood Mill). The spartanburghistory.org page did not open.

### South Dakota
- South Dakota had no leads layers when its first batch was researched; since November 19, 2026 it has all three (35 mapped ruins, the vanished places and the mines), so `start.py` now lists leads there beside the Register list rows.
- siouxfalls.gov answers 403; Experience Sioux Falls (`experiencesiouxfalls.com/fallspark`) and the Siouxland Heritage Museums pages (`siouxlandmuseums.com/?p=111`, `?p=130`) give history, hours, parking and fees. cityofgarretson.com shows a browser challenge.
- NPGallery forms read well with `page.py --all -n 200000` (10 of 11; 84003285 is a placeholder); a small script that splits the cached text into sentences and greps them saves reading whole forms.
- Drawn by script (`page.py` gets nothing): prairiehomestead.com, the Old Fort Meade Museum's Google Sites page, the nps.gov/mimi hours page; plan on `hours='?'` or find a state or NPS page with the times. The Commons user w_lemay has 2023 photographs of most downtown Sioux Falls buildings.
- `fws.gov/locations/dc-booth-national-fish-hatchery/tours` works (the old "visit-us" address is 404). 1881courthousemuseum.com and the Custer city and chamber sites read; the Episcopal Asset Map (`episcopalassetmap.org/node/5081`) read with `hours.py page`.
- Refusing: discoverlaura.org (resets), travelsouthdakota.com (403), custersd.com ("please wait"), stjosephcathedral.net, episcopalchurchsd.org (a browser challenge; November 24, 2026).

### Tennessee
- tnstateparks.com park pages read fully with `page.py --all` (their `/history` sub-pages answer 404).
- The ArcGIS geocoder found places `nom.py` did not ("Stonecipher/Kelly Home", "RM Brooks Store", "Leach Cemetery"): `tools/geo.py "<name>, Rugby, TN"`.
- Tennessee Historical Commission pages (`tn.gov/historicalcommission/state-programs/state-historic-sites/<slug>.html`) give address and hours cleanly (carter-house, sam-houston-schoolhouse, tipton-haynes); `fort-loudoun` and `tellico-blockhouse` are 404, being state parks.
- tnstateparks.com hides hours in the gift shop and "Contact Information" blocks: `page.py --all <url> | grep -o '.\{160\}Hours.\{200\}'`.
- The 2020 Downtown Chattanooga Historic District form (tn.gov PDF, 75 pages) reads with `page.py --all -n 900000` and covers most downtown blocks.
- oaklandsmuseum.org (no connection) and the nps.gov/grsm Elkmont pages (404) failed, archives too.

### Texas
- Big Bend: OpenStreetMap is well mapped there. `nom.py` found most of the historic sites by name ("Dorgan house ruin", "Luna Jacal", "Homer Wilson Ranch", the "Old Sam Nail Ranch" water pump), and `osmnear.py ... buildings -g '.'` found the Hot Springs store, motel and house, tagged `building=ruins`.
- Far West Texas: the pin check's LiDAR sheet (`--lidar`) worked there, and showed the Mariscal Mine works and the lines at Johnson's Ranch better than the aerial did.
- Texas Historical Commission: `thc.texas.gov/historic-sites/<name>` pages give hours and fees at the top with `--all`, under short names (`kreische-brewery` and `monument-hill` work, `monument-hill-kreische-brewery` is 404); Fort Griffin's history page is `thc.texas.gov/state-historic-sites/fort-griffin/fort-griffin-history` and its visitors guide PDF lists every ruin and reconstruction. Its marker pages (`atlas.thc.texas.gov/Details/<n>`) give the text after "Marker Text" (some, like Pemberton Castle, only address and year).
- Register forms: where NPGallery gives the placeholder (Fort Worth; Eckhardt Stores, 76002020), the THC Atlas copies (`https://atlas.thc.state.tx.us/NR/pdfs/<ref>/<ref>.pdf`) have text layers; nine took over six minutes in one shell call, so loop writing each to a file, a few per call.
- TPWD overview pages (`tpwd.texas.gov/state-parks/<park>`) give hours and fees near the end of `--all` (the "fees-facilities" subpage returns almost nothing).
- SAH Archipedia `sah-archipedia.org/buildings/TX-02-FW<n>` gives Fort Worth buildings' address, date, architect and present use. historicfortworth.org answers 403 but read with the web fetch tool.
- Churches: smcaustin.org and austindiocese.org answer 403 to both tools; `austindiocese.news` gave the daily Mass times. The Diocese of Victoria (painted churches) answers 403 with no archive copy; schulenburgchamber.org (the painted-church tours) 403 too.
- Refusing or empty: hhs.texas.gov (403; HHSC releases on `content.govdelivery.com` read), dallasscottishrite.org (no visiting hours), oldred.org (no answer), rosenberg-library.org (drawn by script).

### Utah
- The 1980 Register forms give owner and "accessible" lines, the quickest source of land ownership for a ruin when the BLM site refuses `page.py`.
- Register points for camps and airfields are often a field or runway: `osmnear.py ... -g 'hangar|museum'` first (for Wendover it found the Enola Gay hangar and the museum). The federal land map holds few places: state parks show, but private nonprofit sites (Topaz, Grafton, Iosepa) come back "not on mapped public land".
- Wendover's site is wendoverairfield.org (historicwendoverairfield.com does not exist), 403. dupinternational.org (the DUP museum directory), parowan.org's Old Rock Church page and wchsutah.org would not open; the county's washco.utah.gov pages answered.

### Vermont
- The Vermont Historical Society's county museum directories (`vermonthistory.org/lamoille-county-museum-directory`, `.../essex-and-orleans-county-museum-directory`) give small museums' hours, fees and addresses in one page (the Old Stone House, whose own site is 403, Holland, Island Pond, Barton, Derby, Craftsbury).
- The State Historic Sites hours page drops a site's dates once its season ends; the opening day came from a WCAX story.
- The NPS "Central Vermont" itinerary pages (`nps.gov/nr/travel/centralvermont/cvNN.htm`) are all 404. railtrails.vermont.gov and stonemillvt.com answer 403; vermontbridges.org county pages are 404.

### Virginia
- DHR register pages (`dhr.virginia.gov/historic-registers/<id>/`) give a short history; Virginia Wildlife Resources' birding-trail pages give parking directions and day-use hours for public lands.
- historicstjohnschurch.org is drawn by script; the copy at `/home-1` (found by a web search) reads fully.
- Refusing: genuinesmithfieldva.com business pages (404), stlukesmuseum.org (403), prestwould.org (resets).

### Washington
- Washington State Parks pages give hours, Discover Pass fees and interior hours, with a sourced "park history" page (`/about/news-center/field-guide-blog/<park>-history`) and museum hours by season; the old `/find-parks/state-parks/fort-columbia-historical-state-park` is 404, `parks.wa.gov/506/Fort-Columbia` works.
- Seattle: the Register list (232 rows) does not name most downtown office buildings, so read each building's article; PCAD (`pcad.lib.washington.edu`) covers demolished and unlisted buildings with dates, address and fate. stjames-cathedral.org came back nearly empty (503 to the web fetch tool).
- Tacoma's Register of Historic Places PDF (cms.tacoma.gov, "publication landmarks register") reads with `page.py --all -n 200000`: address, year and architect for about 200 landmarks. parkstacoma.gov and tacomaartslive.org answer 403.
- Walla Walla 2020 (ww2020.net) publishes a spreadsheet of all its building reports (linked at the foot of each, `wp-content/uploads/ww2020_database-research-<date>.xlsx`): title, address and date for several hundred buildings. wallawallawa.gov answers 403, so the 2021 downtown district nomination was not read.
- jchsmuseum.org is a Wix site: its old `/Sites/...` addresses are 404; the live ones are `/museum` and `/visit/historic-houses/`.

### West Virginia
- WV State Parks pages (`wvstateparks.com/park/<name>/`) give the address and little else; WV Tourism's company pages (`wvtourism.com/company/<name>`) carried Carnifex Ferry's museum hours with `--all`.
- Refusing: camdenpark.com, swisshistoricalvillage.org (403), ritchiehistoricalsociety.com (502), sweetspringsresortpark.org (refused), with no readable archive copies.

### Wisconsin
- Register and Wikipedia points in rural Wisconsin landed in crop fields (First Capitol, Taliesin, Gratiot House, 130 to 250 m off); `nom.py` found the buildings. The Tower Hill shot tower's own point is just outside the park on the federal land map, so the pin check says CHECK ACCESS though the park owns it.
- The Wisconsin Historical Society site pages (`pendarvis.wisconsinhistory.org`) read, but their "plan your visit" pages answer 404: the home page carries the season. taliesinpreservation.org answers 403 (American Players Theatre's "Area Guide Spotlight" posts describe the tours); badgermineandmuseum.com is drawn by script.
- The Wisconsin Historical Society property records (`page.py --all -n 60000 https://www.wisconsinhistory.org/Records/Property/HI<n>`; find the number by web search, as their own search page is 404) give year built, walls, architect, other names, "Demolished" yes or no and later surveys the 1985 forms lack (two downtown Superior Register blocks now gone; the Michel brewery torn down). Downtown Superior's numbers run in order along Tower Avenue (HI17825 to HI17864). Its yearly season releases (`wisconsinhistory.org/Records/Article/CS...`) give dates and hours when Villa Louis and Wade House pages say only "closed, reopening May 2027".
- NPGallery forms read well from 1974 on, but some (St. Nazianz chapel, Kiel Mill, Hillsboro) are placeholders. Most mill and barn Register points are on private farms; the pin check could not match four of sixteen to a building, so those stayed leads.
- The Wisconsin Library Heritage Center's "razed Carnegies" page lists 14 gone buildings. Michael Barera's August 2026 La Crosse photographs on Commons cover downtown and campus.
- miltonhouse.org's home page reset the connection but `/tours` read; Circus World is `circusworld.wisconsinhistory.org` (circusworldbaraboo.org gave 526). Refusing: thebasilica.org, city.milwaukee.gov, pabsttheatergroup.com (403/406), superiorpublicmuseums.org (503 to both tools; Fairlawn's hours unchecked); zenithcity.com's Superior pages are 404.

### Wyoming
- Wyoming State Parks pages carry about 9,000 characters of menu: read with `page.py --all -n 40000`, cut at the last "Our Apps", and grep "Hours" (hours sit after "Hours . ." on the "about-" page). Pages open at `wyoparks.wyo.gov/index.php/places-to-go/<site>`; the `about-<site>` pattern worked for Point of Rocks and Names Hill but was 404 for Ames Monument and the Governors' Mansion.
- The Carbon County Visitors Council pages (wyomingcarboncounty.com, snowmobileinwyoming.com) give hours, address and coordinates for every county museum in one line each (`page.py -g 'Open|Address'`). WyoHistory.org field-trip pages (`/field-trips/<name>`) gave access notes (Sweetwater County Museum's tipple page is 404).
- NPS `planyourvisit/<name>.htm` guesses and wyoleg.gov (drawn by script) did not read; a web search result quoting the page gave the Capitol's hours.
